Discharge for the period Oct. 1, 2017, to Mar. 13, 2024, was revised on Nov. 22, 2024, based on changes to the stage-discharge rating.

SANDY CREEK NEAR ADAMS NY, USGS site 04250750 (USGS-04250750), stream in Jefferson County, New York, draining 137 square miles. Daily means from 1957-08-22 to 2026-10-12, a page a water year (October to September), linked below. Discharge in cubic feet a second, gage height in feet above the gauge's datum. Its annual peaks are its floods; its field measurements are the visits that check the gauge. Revisions are USGS's own notes of the site's published history, as written.
